D5 request/decoded-workspace audit checkpoint (2026-10-09): inspected actual preparation -> encoder -> client -> request decoder -> refresh decoder -> awaited Stage sink -> normalization/seal/Store/cleanup lifetimes. Encoder retains record buffers, concatenates the complete request, and validates it before returning; client copies the encoded bytes and passes aliased line views to the drain-aware writer. Writing is incremental but request ownership is not released record-by-record. D5 read-owner checkpoint clarification (2026-10-08): readNativeAnalysisState accepts an optional sixth trusted scalar-record observer (BASELINE_READ_SQL_OWNERSHIP_ONLY), independent of its fifth cumulative READ observer. VALIDATED_BASELINE accounts original header/digest/summary fields plus the read frame's selected state identity/revision-kind/snapshot columns and reused pointer, without duplicating aliased header or wrapper strings. TOKEN_ROW adds only the current six-column raw row and previously accepted three-field token tuples; TOKEN_ACCEPTED additionally owns the current parsed digest but does not count provider/snapshot strings twice while they alias that raw row. TOKEN_RETURN drops prior raw-row columns and retains tuples/pointer. STATE_CLONE_BEGIN/END mark the real clone/freeze boundary, retaining the original SQL field charge once; trusted expectation and clone workspace are not reclassified as new SQL representations. Existing detached Stage-context charging remains unchanged. RETURN is emitted after the read transaction closure has returned and counts only returned state/token fields, not its discarded frame columns. ## Approved Phase1 CI input portability — 2026-11-03

Authority: [integration readiness amendment](../superpowers/specs/2026-11-03-phase1-integration-readiness-proposal.md), explicitly approved with local fixed commit. The existing `p1-syntax-contract-v1` adds `ci-portability/` input only (no new Fixture ID). Historical original byte pins and exact source successor patches remain checked by their original behavior consumers; four additive manifest extensions preserve old fields/criticalFacts/qualityGates. Whole-repository 70/44 discovery/uniqueness and exact 54-path format-exception SHA checks belong to `packages/evaluation/test/fixture-registry.test.mjs`. Source, error, budget, SQL audit, publication and existing quality gates remain in their original tests. Full ordinary-root verification uses a test-owned source projection without private evidence; results and original failures are recorded by CURRENT_STATUS and the original B result index. This grants no Native/profile/phase exit or hosted-CI authorization.

Private Evaluation SQL compatibility: user approved [exact SQL10/13 amendment](../superpowers/specs/2026-11-03-phase1-evaluation-schema-compatibility-proposal.md). Existing p05-code-intelligence-evaluation-v1 registers inputs/schema-compatibility.json (six independent version/query-only cases, no new ID); evaluation-retained-state and original pinned-sqlite real Apply/golden/reopen tests cover the changed reader. Exact unsupported versions, query-only, budgets and Native admission remain gates. Accurate RED/GREEN and first-root failures remain in the original B integrationPreparation result index.
